组合实验\n- 其他 PREPARING/QUEUED 是历史遗留（killed/failed 的旧版本），可忽略\n\n**无等待中的 Worker 回调或 Cron。无待处理的 Director 决策。**\n\n### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）\n- **立即（GPU 空闲 = 失职）** → 优先提交 plan_007 full benchmark（P0，lm-eval ## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单：**cuda:0 + cuda:1** on westd-34887，禁用其他设备
- KL PPL 全文统一引用 **4-seed mean 12.00±0.07**（TEAL sparse PPL），禁用单 seed 最佳值 11.91
- BCE framing 必须用 **"objective mismatch"**，禁止 "anti-learning"
- SPON framing 必须用 **"marginal complement"**，禁止 "KL subsumes SPON"
- s456 outlier：报 "5-seed mean: 12.32±0.71" + "Excluding s456: 12.00±0.07"，不跑第 6 seed
- submit_training_job 命令必须自带环境激活（`source setup_env.sh && ...` 或 `source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && ...`）
- EMNLP 2026 ARR May DDL **2026-05-25**，剩 10 天

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：**westd-34887**，账号 root
- 工作目录：`/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/`
- Python/conda：需 SSH 探测确认（上次用 `source setup_env.sh` 或 miniconda3）
- 模型：Llama-3.1-8B（路径需探测），Mistral-7B 在 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/`
- Checkpoints：`/root/distill_sparse_swiglu/checkpoints/` 下有 mvp_kl_norm_v2_s42, spon_kl_s42, spon_2048_s42, bce_comp_staged_s42, bce_isocompute_v2_s42, kl_sparsity70_s42, kl_sparsity30_s42
- 活跃进程：**无**（reconcile_crons=0，无 RUNNING 实验）
- W&B：需探测确认 project name

### 3. 📊 当前状态
**所有实验都停了，0 个 RUNNING，0 个活跃 cron。GPU 全部空闲。**

已完成的核心实验（plan_001-006 + plan_008 部分）：
- Claim 1 (KL >> all)：✅ 三层梯队确认。KL 12.00 >> training-free 23-28 >> BCE 54.84。STE 15.77。
- Claim 2 (SPON orthogonal)：✅ SPON recovery 仅 7.3%，predictor effect >> SPON correction (482 vs 76)
- Claim 3 (sparsity scaling)：🔄 30% 和 70% 训练完成，但 eval 数据不完整（70% TEAL PPL=136K 异常，疑似 normalization bug D017）

PREPARING 但未提交的实验队列：
- **mistral_kl_s42**（plan_009 P1）：Mistral 跨架构验证，代码已确认零改动兼容
- **bce_isocompute_s42_v2**：BCE iso-compute retry（上一次 step 500 crash）
- **eval_spon_supp_retry**：SPON 补充 eval
- **spon_kl_s42**：SPON+KL 组合实验
- 其他 PREPARING/QUEUED 是历史遗留（killed/failed 的旧版本），可忽略

**无等待中的 Worker 回调或 Cron。无待处理的 Director 决策。**

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **立即（GPU 空闲 = 失职）** → 优先提交 plan_007 full benchmark（P0，lm-eval 脚本据说就绪）和 plan_009 mistral_kl_s42（P1），两块卡并行跑
- plan_008 70% sparsity TEAL PPL=136K 异常（D017）→ 派 Worker 排查 evaluate.py normalization bug → 修复后重新 eval 70% 和 30% 的 TEAL PPL
- plan_007 lm-eval 结果出来 → 汇总 WikiText-2/C4/ARC-c/WinoGrande/MMLU/GSM8K → 更新论文 Table 1
- mistral_kl_s42 完成 → 与 Llama 对比 → 若 KL 同样显著优于 training-free，Claim 1 泛化性成立
- BCE objective mismatch 分析 → 需写入论文 analysis section（memory.md 已有完整分析）
- bce_isocompute_s42_v2 和 eval_spon_supp_retry → 视 GPU 空闲插入
- R-Sparse normalization 修复 → 低优先级，appendix only

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心结论链**：KL distillation 使 SwiGLU contextual sparsity prediction 不需要 error compensation 和 static bias correction。三层梯队（KL >> training-free >> BCE）和 SPON marginal complement 是两个已确认的 claim。

**DDL 压力**：距 2026-05-25 仅 10 天。plan_007 full benchmark 是论文最大缺口（目前只有 WikiText-2 PPL），必须最高优先级。plan_009 Mistral 泛化是加分项但非必须。

**易错点**：
1. 70% sparsity TEAL PPL=136K 是 bug 不是真实数据——evaluate.py 的 per-layer normalization 可能没覆盖 predictor path（D017），不要把这个数字写进论文
2. kl_sparsity30_s42_v3 实际收敛到 28.5%（不是 30%），已决策接受（D015/D016），报告时标注 "achieved sparsity"
3. PREPARING 队列里有很多 killed/failed 的旧实验残留（mvp_kl_s456, mvp_kl_s123, kl_lagrangian_v2 等），状态为 PREPARING 但 conclusion=killed——这些是历史遗留，不要尝试重跑
4. setup_env.sh 是 westd-34887 上的环境激活脚本——submit_training_job 命令前缀用 `source setup_env.sh &&`
5. gradient_checkpointing 对所有 Llama-3.1-8B 训练是必须的，不加则 OOM

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\"}, \"bce_isocompute_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\\\n\\\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\\\n\\\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\\\n\\\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\\\n\\\\n## 根因分析\\\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\\\n\\\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\\\", BCE treats each neuron as an independent binary classification, losing inter-neuron coordination and output distribution signal. The predictor barely learned meaningful patterns (BCE only 5% better than random). KL's direct output-distribution matching provides much stronger learning signal. Subsumption Test Results (50 samples, seq_len=2048)\\n\\n### 核心指标\\n- Linearity ratio: 0.264 ± 0.007 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n- Magnitude ratio: 0.158 ± 0.009 (SPON correction / predictor effect)\\n- Cosine similarity: 0.196 ± 0.027 (near-orthogonal)\\n\\n### Effect magnitudes\\n- ‖D(predictor)‖ = 482.65 (KL predictor logit shift)\\n- ‖D(SPON)‖ = 76.20 (SPON bias correction logit shift)\\n- ‖interaction‖ = 147.74 (cross-layer cascade non-linearity)\\n\\n### 结论：Informative — SPON corrections are orthogonal and negligibly small\\n\\n1. **SPON 与 mask 效应正交**：cos_sim ≈ 0.20，两种修正作用在不同维度\\n2. **SPON 量级微小**：仅为 predictor 效应的 16%（76 vs 483）\\n3. **逐层精确可加**：交互项（148）来源于跨层级联非线性（down_proj 是线性的，层内 mask+bias 严格可加），非层内交互\\n4. **支持 D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing**：SPON 提供的是正交但微弱的补充，不构成有意义的 compositional gain\\n\\n⚠️ 不使用 \\\"subsumption\\\" 措辞——数据显示正交性而非包含关系。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects; the non-zero interaction term arises from cross-layer cascading, not intra-layer coupling.\\n\\n### 产出文件\\nresults/subsumption_test/subsumption_results.json (15KB)\"}, \"bce_isocompute_s42_v2\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T18:35:42.177Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-15T20:03:55.571Z\", \"notes\": \"STE baseline 代码实现完成 (Worker worker_code_ste_baseline_20260515T183635)。修改：src/predictor.py 新增 STEMask 类（hard threshold + STE gradient），src/train.py 新增 --relaxation ste/gumbel 参数。\\n\\n## WikiText-2 评估 (seq_len=2048, 141 sequences, calibration=32)\\n- True Dense PPL: 6.12\\n- Predictor Hard Mask PPL: 8.18 (per-token sparsity=0.470, 低于 target 0.5)\\n- TEAL Sparse PPL: **15.77** (global 50% sparsity)\\n\\n## vs Gumbel-sigmoid 对比 (TEAL Sparse PPL, 公平 50% 全局稀疏度)\\n| 指标 | STE | Gumbel 4-seed mean | 差异 |\\n|------|-----|---------------------|------|\\n| TEAL Sparse PPL | 15.77 | 12.00 ± 0.07 | +31.4% |\\n| Hard Mask PPL | 8.18 | 9.19 ± 0.04 | -11.0%* |\\n| Per-token sparsity | 0.470 | 0.498 | -2.8pp |\\n\\n*Hard mask PPL 不可直接比较：STE per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（低于 Gumbel 0.498），较低稀疏度自然带来较低 PPL。\\n\\n## Lambda 震荡统计\\nSTE hard threshold 导致 Lagrangian multiplier 剧烈震荡：\\n- Steps 70-130 (关键不稳定区间): mean=66.40, std=94.87, range=[0.01, 1000]\\n- 前 500 步 (51 pts): mean=688.07, std=440.57, min=0.01, max=1000\\n- 后 500 步 (51 pts): mean=965.69, std=141.59, min=250, max=1000\\n- 全程: mean=826.88, std=354.23\\n\\nLambda 在前 100 步内从 0.01 飙升到 1000 再回落到 3.91，反复震荡。对比 Gumbel 训练中 lambda 平稳收敛到 cap，STE 的 constraint 满足极不稳定。\\n\\n## 根因分析\\nSTE hard threshold (>0.5=keep, ≤0.5=prune) 导致：\\n1. 离散 sparsity 值（sigmoid 微小变化可翻转大量 neuron）→ constraint 满足不稳定\\n2. Lambda 被迫在 0.01-1000 间震荡以追踪快速变化的 constraint violation\\n3. 最终收敛到 lambda=1000 (cap) 但 per-token sparsity 仅 0.470（未达 target 0.5）\\n4. 逐层稀疏度极端两极化（Layer 0-17: 5-56%, Layer 18-31: 56-90%），导致 TEAL 全局分配效果差\\n\\n## Conclusion: SUCCESS (验证 Gumbel 必要性)\\nTEAL sparse PPL 15.77 显著差于 Gumbel 12.00 (+31.4%)，确认 Gumbel soft mask 的平滑梯度对 KL distillation 训练至关重要。可直接用于 Reviewer Concern #3 defense。\", - **plan_009** (P1, Mistral): mistral_kl_s42 is registered but waiting to be submitted.

I've got cuda:0 and cuda:1 available on westd-34887, so I can run plan_007's full benchmark evaluation across the missing tasks while simultaneously training the Mistral KL distillation on the other GPU.

Meanwhile, I'll spin up a CPU worker to debug that TEAL PPL anomaly at 70% sparsity. Let me just get these workers started—they can SSH in and figure out what's actually available on the server. For plan_007, I need to evaluate the best KL-only checkpoint against the training-free baselines on the standard benchmarks, which is purely evaluation work so it should fit cleanly on one GPU.

For plan_009 with Mistral, I'll need to locate the model at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/mistral-7b/` and verify the training script handles the Mistral architecture properly before running the same KL-normalized setup at 50% sparsity. Let me also check memory.md to see if there's anything else about the lm-eval configuration I should know.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_14/projects/distill_sparse_swiglu/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Project Memory\\n\\n## Paper Framing Decisions (Director-approved)\\n\\n### BCE vs training-free: \\\"objective mismatch\\\" 非 \\\"anti-learning\\\" (2026-05-16)\\nBCE (54.84) 比 training-free (23-33) 差。**禁止用 \\\"anti-learning\\\" framing**，改用 **\\\"objective mismatch\\\"**：BCE 优化 per-neuron binary accuracy，TEAL 推理用 global top-k 重分配，两者结构性不兼容。BCE 学到的 per-neuron scoring 在 global budget allocation 下不是最优的。正确 claim: loss function 必须匹配推理管线的 sparsity allocation 机制。KL 直接优化 output distribution，天然兼容任何 allocation 策略。\\n\\n### KL PPL 统一引用规范 (2026-05-16)\\n论文中所有 KL PPL 对比统一用 **4-seed mean 12.00±0.07**（TEAL sparse PPL），不要用单 seed 最佳值 11.91。BCE iso-compute 对比时标注步数（1000 步 vs KL 1000 步，但 BCE per-step FLOPs 仅 KL 的 1/2.71）。\\n\\n### SPON framing: \\\"marginal complement\\\" 非 \\\"subsumption\\\" (2026-05-15)\\n禁止 \\\"KL subsumes SPON\\\" 措辞。正确表述：SPON corrections are orthogonal to and much smaller than predictor mask effects (cos_sim=0.196, magnitude ratio=16%)。D010 \\\"marginal complement\\\" framing。\\n\\n### s456 outlier 报告方式 (2026-05-16)\\n报 \\\"5-seed mean: 12.32±0.71\\\" + \\\"Excluding s456 (TEAL allocation outlier): 12.00±0.07\\\"。附 per-seed breakdown table + Layer 31 keep ratio 分析。不跑第 6 个 seed。\\n\\n## Key Insights (non-metrics)\\n\\n### KL predictor implicitly subsumes static correction (2026-05-15)\\nSPON bias vectors recover only 3.8% of sparse-vs-dense gap on top of KL predictor (PPL 9.22→9.10). Root cause: KL's end-to-end gradient already captures average-case AND per-input neuron importance through d_logits/dh * dh/dm coupling. Static bias correction is redundant when the contextual predictor is trained with output-distribution alignment.\\n\\n### SPON seq_len=2048 confirms subsumption, rules out mismatch confound (2026-05-15)\\nspon_2048_s42: training seq_len matched eval seq_len=2048. Recovery improved 3.8%→7.3% (+3.5pp), bias norm 0.041→0.053 (+30%), but still < 10% threshold. Even under matched conditions, static bias is fundamentally limited against KL predictor's per-token mask.\\n\\n### Compensation cannot fix BCE's fundamental flaw (2026-05-15)\\nBCE+Compensation (67.2M params, staged training) only improves per-token PPL by 9% (27.96→25.44), still 2.8x worse than KL-only (9.21). TEAL global path completely unaffected by comp network (44.54 = BCE-only). Root cause: BCE gradient dL/ds_j is per-neuron factorized.\\n\\n### Training-free baselines: per-layer normalization is critical (2026-05-16)\\nCRITICAL bug in _global_topk_masks: activation magnitudes vary 50-100x across layers. Fix: divide each layer's importance scores by layer mean before global topk. Corrected PPL: Vanilla TEAL 23.31, WINA 27.93, TEAL Greedy 33.47. R-Sparse still broken (SVD scores need percentile/max normalization). **Same bug likely affects predictor TEAL path** — sparsity70 TEAL PPL=136K anomaly.\\n\\n### BCE objective mismatch with TEAL inference (2026-05-16)\\nBCE iso-compute (1000 steps, reg_weight=2.0, sparsity=0.502) gets TEAL PPL=54.84 — worse than all 3 training-free baselines (23-33). Root cause: BCE optimizes per-neuron binary accuracy independently, but TEAL inference uses global top-k budget allocation across layers. Per-neuron scores optimized for binary accuracy are structurally incompatible with global budget allocation. KL directly optimizes output distribution, naturally compatible with any allocation strategy.\\n\\n### Hard-mask PPL vs TEAL sparse PPL variance separation (2026-05-15)\\n5-seed hard-mask PPL std = 0.04 (9.14-9.25), 极其稳定。TEAL sparse PPL std = 0.69 (11.91-13.58), 方差大 16 倍。方差来自 TEAL 全局分配算法与 per-token mask 的匹配度。\\n\\n### KL vs BCE per-step FLOPs ratio = 2.71x (2026-05-15)\\n实测 (BS=1, SL=512): KL 0.549s/step, BCE 0.202s/step。Iso-compute BCE 需要 2700 步 (= 1000 × 2.71) 匹配 KL 1000 步的总 FLOPs。\\n\\n### gradient_checkpointing 对 sparsity training 是必须的 (2026-05-15)\\n所有 LLaMA-3.1-8B sparsity 训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing。不加则 OOM（>96GB），加了后 46.9-67.6GB。\\n\\n### BCE iso-compute sparsity 收敛需要 reg_weight=2.0 (2026-05-15)\\n原始 SparsityRegularizer 在 torch.no_grad() 下计算 mask，梯度为零。修复：STE hard mask + reg_weight=2.0。\\n\\n### lambda_max=1000 对低 sparsity target 不够 (2026-05-16)\\nsparsity30 (target=0.30) 两次 sparsity 卡在 0.281，lambda 撞 1000 cap。v3 用 lambda_max=5000。\\n\\n### Mistral-7B 与 Llama-3.1-8B 代码完全兼容 (2026-05-16)\\ntrain.py 零改动即可运行。架构一致。Predictor 参数量完全相同 (~75.96M)。vocab_size 差异只影响 KL loss logit 维度。\\n\\n## Checkpoints\\n- KL-only predictor (s42): --gradient_checkpointing --seed 42 --output_dir checkpoints/mistral_kl_s42\"\n```\n\n### Phase 3: Dry run 验证\n先跑 2-3 步确认：\n- 模型加载正常\n- Predictor 初始化正常\n- KL loss 值在合理范围（~1.0-2.0 初始）\n- 无 OOM\n\n### Phase 4: 提交训练\n确认 dry run 成功后，使用 `submit_training_job` 提交完整训练：\n- server: westd-34887\n- gpu_ids: [\"cuda:1\"]\n- exp_id: mistral_kl_s42\n- plan_id: plan_009\n- project: distill_sparse_swiglu\n- command: 上面构造的完整命令（必须 bash -c \"...\" 包裹）\n\n### Phase 5: 更新 Registry\n用 update_registry 更新 mistral_kl_s42 的 spec 字段（provenance）：\n- command, server, data_path, data_count, model_name, seed\n\n## 注意事项\n- 命令必须用 bash -c \"...\" 包裹（tmux 用 sh 不是 bash）\n- gradient_checkpointing 是必须的，不加则 OOM\n- GPU 编号始终用 0（CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 由框架注入映射到物理 cuda:1）\n- 训练约 17-30 分钟完成\n- 训练完成后 cron 系统会自动回调，不需要轮询\n\n## 产出\n1. 确认训练已提交并正在运行\n2. Registry 更新（status=RUNNING, spec 填写完整）",

完整读取\ncat src/baselines.py | grep -A 30 \"_global_topk\"  # 看已修复的 baselines 版本\n```\n\n重点关注：\n1. evaluate.py 中 TEAL sparse 推理的 mask 生成逻辑\n2. predictor sigmoid 输出如何转化为 global topk mask\n3. 是否有 per-layer normalization 步骤\n4. 对比 baselines.py 中已修复的 `_global_topk_masks`\n\n### Phase 2: 诊断\n确认 bug 根因：\n- predictor 输出的 sigmoid 值是否跨层幅度差异大？\n- global topk 是否在没有 normalization 的情况下操作？\n- 修复 baselines.py 时的方法能否直接移植？\n\n### Phase 3: 修复\n如果确认是 normalization 缺失：\n1. 在 evaluate.py 的 TEAL sparse 路径中添加 per-layer mean normalization\n2. 确保修复只影响 TEAL global topk 路径，不影响 per-token hard mask 路径\n3. 写清楚注释说明为什么需要 normalization\n\n### Phase 4: 验证修复\n修复后，用 70% checkpoint 重跑 eval：\n```bash\ncd /root/distill_sparse_swiglu\npython src/evaluate.py --checkpoint_path checkpoints/kl_sparsity70_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt --sparsity_target 0.7 [其他参数]\n```\n\n期望：TEAL sparse PPL 从 136K 降到 20-50 范围。\n\n同时用 30% checkpoint 验证：\n```bash\npython src/evaluate.py --checkpoint_path checkpoints/kl_sparsity30_s42/predictor_kl_normalized.pt --sparsity_target 0.3 [其他参数]\n```\n\n注意：30% checkpoint 实际收敛到 28.5%（不是 30%），在 eval 时注意 sparsity target 设置。\n\n### Phase 5: 更新 Registry\n用 update_registry 更新：\n- kl_sparsity70_s42_v2 的 teal_sparse_ppl（修正值）\n- kl_sparsity30_s42_v3 的 teal_sparse_ppl（新增，之前没 eval）\n\n## 注意事项\n- 先用 ssh_read_only 读代码分析，只在需要修改文件或运行实验时用 ssh_execute\n- 验证 eval 时需要 GPU——目前 cuda:0 被 lm-eval 占用，cuda:1 被 Mistral 训练占用。如果两个 GPU 都忙，先完成代码修复，将修复后的 eval 命令写好留给后续执行\n- 修复的 evaluate.py 必须保持向后兼容（不破坏 50% sparsity 的现有结果）\n\n## 产出\n1.
